* Term Work = Viva / Presentations/ Journals/ assignments/ quizes / MCQs/Business plans/Role plans etc.
** NUE = Non University Examination (examination conducted at school level)
# Syllabus common for B Tech (Mechanical), MBA Tech (Mechanical), can use common question paper.
# Engineering Mechanics - Syllabus is common for B Tech (Mechnaical & Mechatronics) and MBA Tech Mechanical, can use common question paper. f
# Complex Variables and Transforms - Syllabus is common for B Tech (EXTC, Mechanical, Civil and Mechatronics), MBA Tech Mechanical and B Tech Integrated Mechanical, can use common question paper.
** Machine Drawing - course requires testing knowledge of drawing fundamentals along with the competency in a modelling software package. Evaluation is done in NUE mode to test software competency and drawing skills.
Machine Shop-I - course is a practice-oriented course which requires students to build jobs using different materials and machining processes. Evaluation is purely based on periodic assessments based on the jobs prepared by the students.
Community Service - course provides students a real-life hands on experience in the community and sensitizes them towards the societal challenges like poverty, illiteracy, hunger, child labor and inequality.

# Syllabus common for B Tech (Mechanical) and MBA Tech (Mechanical), can use common question paper.

**Industrial Applications of HVAC - course imparts knowledge about HVAC design, control systems, and plant equipment selection. Because it is entirely industry-based, evaluation is completed in NUE mode at the end of the semester.
Machine Shop-II - course is a practice-oriented course. The evaluation is purely based on periodic assessments based on the jobs prepared by the students.
